How Google treats the line finder’s failures

295 crop IDs measured · 236 calls · 277 Anemi files withheld · estimated US$0.3540

Read each native crop against the owner’s observation and Google’s layout lines. A single confident line can still omit or append text. Red inset: silent candidate under the operational rule; it needs visual confirmation.

One card per crop ID, including local-only withheld crops. Primary groups follow catalogue types; secondary comment types appear on the same card. Identical crop bytes share a response. Tap a crop to open its native-resolution image. Images are local files.
